================================================================================
METHODOLOGY
================================================================================
  WIN     : TP hit before SL — no time limit
  LOSS    : SL hit before TP — no time limit
  ONGOING : Trade still open at report generation time
  UNKNOWN : Data fetch error

  F5 HARD REJECT : abs(slope) < 0.10% — immediate discard
  F6 STRICT SIGN : slope_recent < slope_prior < 0 required
  F9 FIXED       : counter resets on WIN, pause vs entry_ts
  DIST ZONES     : 0.20–0.35% (A) and 0.50–0.65% (B)
  LONGs DISABLED : 1W/8T over 5 months — no edge
================================================================================
